# White-Label & Reseller Businesses: 3,067 ideas (part 1 of 3: Activities & Tickets → Franchise-Style Micro-Businesses (coffee carts, kiosks, vending routes))

[← back to index](README.md) · 1024 entries in this file · 109 categories in total

Businesses where you sell someone else's product, service or inventory under your own brand, plus open-source software that could run the operation. Supply sources are starting points for research, so check current partner terms. The regulation column flags where a license or registration is usually needed. Startup cost: Low (1994) · Medium (926) · High (147).

**Parts:** **part 1** (Activities & Tickets → Franchise-Style Micro-Businesses (coffee carts, kiosks, vending routes)) · [part 2](resell-2.md) (Freight Brokering → Reselling Music, Stock Media & Licensing) · [part 3](resell-3.md) (Reselling Parking & Storage Space → eSIM & Travel Data)

## Activities & Tickets

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **City-pass style attraction bundle under own brand** | Multi-attraction admission bundles under own brand | e.g. Tiqets, GetYourGuide supplier API, attractions direct | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Bundle margin on discounted admission | 10-20% bundle margin | Consumer-protection and refund rules only | City hotels and hostels via front-desk partnerships | Medium | Visitors pay for convenience; attractions discount for guaranteed volume |
| **Theme-park discount ticket reseller** | Discounted theme park admission tickets | e.g. authorized ticket wholesalers, park trade programs | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Wholesale-to-retail spread per ticket | 5-12% spread | Authorized-reseller agreement with each park | Families via SEO and deal newsletters | Low | Parks protect gate price; authorized resellers get net rates |
| **Hotel concierge activity storefront** | Local tours and experiences branded for each hotel | e.g. Viator, GetYourGuide affiliate, local operators | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Commission shared with hotel per booking | 8-12% commission, split with hotel | None beyond operator liability insurance | Independent hotels via GM outreach | Low | Hotels want guest upsell revenue without staffing a concierge |
| **Kids' birthday-party experience booking** | Party venue slots and entertainers | e.g. trampoline parks, entertainers, play centres | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Booking commission per party | 15% commission | Child-safety vetting of entertainers | Parents via school Facebook groups and mums' forums | Low | Parents are time-poor; venues have idle weekday-afternoon capacity |
| **Adventure-sports booking marketplace** | Skydiving, rafting and bungee slots | e.g. local adventure operators, Bokun-connected suppliers | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [alextselegidis/easyappointments](https://github.com/alextselegidis/easyappointments) | Commission per booking | 15-20% commission | Operator liability insurance and waivers | Backpackers via hostel partnerships and Instagram | Low | Fragmented operators lack online booking; you aggregate demand |
| **Museum and gallery ticket resale for tour groups** | Group admission tickets with timed entry | e.g. museum group sales desks | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext) | Per-head margin on group rates | 8-15% per head | Group-sales agreement with each museum | Inbound tour operators via trade fairs | Low | Museums give group rates; operators avoid admin of timed entry |
| **Experience-gift voucher company** | Redeemable experience vouchers | e.g. spas, driving tracks, restaurants, operators | [saleor/saleor](https://github.com/saleor/saleor), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Voucher markup plus unredeemed breakage | 20-30% margin plus breakage | Gift-card expiry and unclaimed-property rules | Corporate HR gifting and Christmas shoppers via ads | Medium | Breakage and float make vouchers highly profitable |
| **Corporate team-building activity broker** | Escape rooms, workshops and team events | e.g. local activity vendors, facilitators | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Margin per event booked | 20-30% per event | None | Office managers via LinkedIn outreach | Low | HR wants one invoice and a planner; vendors want corporate volume |
| **Cruise-port shore activity marketplace** | Independent port experiences for cruise passengers | e.g. local tour operators in each port | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission per booking | 20% commission | Operator insurance and port-authority permits | Cruise passengers via SEO on port names | Low | Ship excursions are overpriced; passengers seek cheaper local options |
| **Broadway and West End theatre package reseller** | Show tickets bundled with dinner | e.g. authorized ticket agencies, partner restaurants | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Package markup over ticket face value | 10-15% package margin | Ticket-resale rules (STAR membership in UK) | Tourists and date-night couples via Google Ads | Low | Bundling hides ticket markup and lifts average order value |
| **Golf tee-time reseller** | Discounted tee times at partner courses | e.g. golf courses, tee-time wholesalers | [alextselegidis/easyappointments](https://github.com/alextselegidis/easyappointments), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Booking fee per round | 5-10% per round | None | Golf travellers via golf forums and clubs | Low | Courses dump off-peak slots cheaply; golfers want variety |
| **Wine-tasting tour booking site** | Winery tours and tastings | e.g. wineries, small tour operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop) | Commission per tour booked | 15-20% commission | Alcohol-service licences held by operators | Wine tourists via regional SEO content | Low | Wineries are poor at online sales; regional authority site wins |
| **Cooking-class marketplace in tourist cities** | Cooking classes and market tours | e.g. local chefs, cooking schools | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission per seat | 20% commission | Food-hygiene certification by hosts | Tourists via TripAdvisor listings and hotel partnerships | Low | Chefs want full classes; tourists want authentic food experiences |
| **Fishing charter booking site** | Charter boat trips | e.g. charter captains, marina operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [alextselegidis/easyappointments](https://github.com/alextselegidis/easyappointments) | Commission per charter | 10-15% commission | Captains hold coastguard licences | Anglers via fishing forums and regional SEO | Low | Captains lack marketing; a niche vertical site converts well |
| **White-label activities widget for travel agencies** | Global activity inventory under agency brand | e.g. Viator, Musement, Hotelbeds activities APIs | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | SaaS fee plus margin share | 10-15% margin share | Agency travel licences remain with agency | Small travel agencies via industry associations | Medium | Agencies lack tech; you monetise their customer base at scale |
| **Stadium tour and sports experience reseller** | Stadium tours and matchday hospitality | e.g. clubs, official ticket partners | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission per ticket | 8-12% commission | Official-partner status needed for match tickets | Sports fans via club fan forums | Low | Clubs outsource tours; fans buy on emotion |
| **Ski-pass and lift-ticket reseller for tour groups** | Lift passes and ski-school slots | e.g. resort trade desks, ski-school operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Spread between trade and retail pass price | 8-15% spread | Resort reseller agreement | School and club ski groups via organisers | Low | Groups prebook passes; resorts give trade rates for volume |
| **Hot-air balloon flight booking agency** | Balloon flight seats | e.g. licensed balloon operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission per seat | 15-20% commission | Operators hold aviation operating certificates | Couples and gift buyers via Google Ads | Low | Weather-dependent operators need demand smoothing and deposits |
| **Escape-room multi-venue pass for a city** | Multi-venue escape-room passes | e.g. independent escape-room owners | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [saleor/saleor](https://github.com/saleor/saleor) | Pass margin plus breakage | 15-25% margin plus breakage | None | Corporate teams and hen groups via social ads | Low | Enthusiasts play many rooms; owners fill off-peak slots |
| **Guided hiking and trekking day-trip reseller** | Guided hikes and trekking day trips | e.g. mountain guides, trekking agencies | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop) | Commission per hiker | 15-20% commission | Guide certification and insurance | Hostels and outdoor clubs via partnerships | Low | Guides lack digital marketing; hikers want vetted operators |
| **Water-park and aquarium family bundle site** | Family bundles of water-park and aquarium tickets | e.g. aquariums, water parks trade desks | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Bundle margin per family pack | 10-15% bundle margin | Authorized-reseller agreements | Families via school-holiday SEO content | Low | Families buy combos; attractions pay for incremental visits |
| **Food-tour and street-food crawl booking brand** | Food tours and street-food crawls | e.g. local food-tour operators, chefs | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission per guest | 20% commission | Food-hygiene rules apply to operators | Foodie travellers via Instagram and TikTok | Low | Food tours are high-margin and easy to sell visually |
| **Bachelor and hen-party activity package planner** | Party activity bundles with venues | e.g. activity venues, bars, operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Package margin per group | 15-25% package margin | None | Party organisers via Google Ads and wedding forums | Low | Organisers pay for convenience; venues give group rates |
| **Scuba-diving course and dive-trip reseller** | Dive courses and boat-dive slots | e.g. PADI and SSI dive centres | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission per course or dive | 10-15% commission | Dive centres hold agency affiliation | Dive travellers via dive forums and SEO | Low | Dive centres rely on walk-ins; prebooking fills boats |
| **Northern-lights and stargazing tour aggregator** | Aurora and stargazing tours | e.g. Arctic and desert tour operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop) | Commission per seat | 15-20% commission | None | Winter travellers via SEO on aurora forecasts | Low | Seasonal demand spikes; aggregator captures search intent |
| **Hop-on hop-off bus ticket affiliate storefront** | Sightseeing bus tickets | e.g. City Sightseeing, Big Bus affiliate programs | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Affiliate commission per ticket | 8-12% commission | None | Tourists via city-guide content sites | Low | Content SEO around city guides converts to bus tickets |
| **Zoo and safari-park membership reseller for employers** | Discounted annual passes as employee perks | e.g. zoos, safari parks membership desks | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Margin per membership sold | 8-12% margin | None | HR benefits managers via LinkedIn outreach | Low | Employers want cheap perks; zoos want recurring members |
| **Festival and concert camping add-on reseller** | Festival camping and glamping upgrades | e.g. glamping operators, festival organisers | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Markup per camping package | 15-25% markup | Campsite licences held by operators | Festival-goers via festival partnerships | Low | Organisers outsource camping; attendees pay for comfort |
| **Photography-tour and workshop booking site** | Photo walks and workshops | e.g. professional photographers, tour guides | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission per participant | 20% commission | None | Hobby photographers via camera-club newsletters | Low | Photographers want bookings; hobbyists pay premium for expertise |
| **Ghost-tour and haunted-walk booking brand** | Evening walking tours | e.g. independent tour guides | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop) | Commission per ticket | 20-25% commission | Local guiding licences in some cities | Tourists via TripAdvisor and hotel leaflets | Low | High-margin tours with near-zero operator cost |
| **Motorsport track-day experience reseller** | Supercar driving experiences and track days | e.g. race circuits, driving-experience operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission per driver | 10-15% commission | Operator insurance and circuit rules | Gift buyers via Father's Day campaigns | Low | Gift-driven demand; operators seek fill for weekday sessions |
| **Airbnb-host activity upsell widget** | Local experiences embedded in host guidebooks | e.g. Viator, GetYourGuide affiliate feeds | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Commission split with hosts | 8-12% commission, host share | Short-term rental rules unaffected | Superhosts via host Facebook groups | Low | Hosts gain income; guests trust host recommendations |

## Airport Transfers

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Branded airport transfer booking site for one city** | Private and shared airport transfers | e.g. Mozio, HolidayTaxis, local licensed taxi firms | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Markup per transfer booked | 15-25% markup | Operators hold private-hire licences | Inbound tourists via SEO on airport name | Low | Airport-name SEO is cheap; operators pay for prepaid demand |
| **White-label transfer widget for hotels** | Guest transfers booked under hotel brand | e.g. local chauffeur companies, transfer wholesalers | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Revenue share with hotels per booking | 10-20% commission, hotel share | Operator licensing only | Independent hotels via hospitality associations | Low | Hotels monetise arrivals; guests trust hotel-branded booking |
| **Meet-and-greet VIP fast-track service** | Airport VIP assist and fast-track passes | e.g. airport VIP lounges, fast-track providers | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Per-passenger margin | 20-30% per passenger | Airport concession agreements held by providers | Luxury travel agents and executive assistants | Low | High-ticket service; airports outsource to concession holders |
| **Cruise-port shuttle reseller** | Port-to-airport shuttle seats | e.g. coach operators, port shuttle firms | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Per-seat markup | 15-20% per seat | Coach operator licences | Cruise passengers via cruise forums and SEO | Low | Predictable sailing schedules make capacity easy to presell |
| **Ski-resort transfer bookings** | Mountain shuttle seats and private transfers | e.g. Alpine transfer operators (Alps2Alt, local firms) | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission per transfer | 10-20% commission | Operators hold winter-driving and PHV licences | Ski travellers via chalet companies and forums | Low | Seasonal peak demand; chalet partners refer every guest |
| **Corporate ground-transport account management** | Chauffeured rides on monthly invoice | e.g. limo affiliates, black-car networks | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Monthly invoicing margin per ride | 10-15% margin | Chauffeur licences held by affiliates | Executive assistants at law and finance firms | Medium | Companies want one invoice and duty-of-care; affiliates want volume |
| **Wheelchair-accessible transfer booking** | Accessible vans with trained drivers | e.g. accessible transport firms | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Booking fee per trip | 10-15% per trip | Accessibility and PHV regulations | Disability travel agents and care homes | Low | Underserved niche with loyal repeat customers |
| **Group-event shuttle coordination** | Event shuttle buses and minibuses | e.g. coach and minibus operators | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Per-event coordination fee plus markup | 15-20% per event | Coach operator licences | Wedding planners and conference organisers | Low | Organisers need one coordinator; operators avoid planning work |
| **Helicopter airport transfer reseller** | Helicopter shuttle seats | e.g. helicopter charter operators | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission per seat | 10% commission | Operators hold air operator certificates | Luxury concierges and private-jet travellers | Low | Ultra-high ticket; concierges refer for reliability |
| **Pet-travel airport transport service** | Pet taxi and cargo handling | e.g. pet transport firms, IPATA agents | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Per-trip margin | 15-25% per trip | Animal transport regulations (IATA LAR) | Relocating expats via relocation agencies | Low | Stressful niche; owners pay for specialists |
| **Airport parking aggregator** | Off-airport parking spots | e.g. park-and-ride lots, hotel car parks | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Commission per parking booking | 15-25% commission | None | Drivers via airport-parking SEO comparisons | Low | Lots have spare capacity; comparison sites win search |
| **Luggage-forwarding and storage reseller** | Luggage shipping and storage | e.g. luggage couriers (Luggage Forward), storage networks | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Per-bag margin | 15-20% per bag | Courier insurance and customs rules | Cruise and ski travellers via travel agents | Low | Travellers pay to skip bag fees and hassle |
| **Airline-partner transfer add-on API** | Ancillary transfer inventory for airline checkout | e.g. transfer wholesalers (Mozio, HolidayTaxis) | [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Wholesale-to-retail spread per transfer | 10-15% spread | Airline partnership contracts | Low-cost airlines' ancillary teams | Medium | Airlines sell ancillaries at scale with zero inventory risk |
| **Night-arrival safe-ride for students** | Vetted late-night student pickups | e.g. local licensed drivers | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Per-ride fee charged to universities | 15-20% per ride | Driver background checks and PHV licences | University international offices | Low | Universities fund safety; parents value vetted drivers |
| **Train-station transfer and porter bundles** | Station pickups bundled with porters | e.g. local chauffeur firms, porter services | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Bundle markup | 15-20% markup | PHV licences | Rail travellers with luggage via rail-deal sites | Low | Bundling porter service differentiates from taxi apps |
| **Shared airport shuttle seat reseller for hostels** | Shared shuttle seats sold through hostels | e.g. shuttle van operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Per-seat commission | 10-15% per seat | Operator PHV licences | Hostel front desks via Hostelworld-listed hostels | Low | Budget travellers prebook cheap seats; hostels earn commission |
| **Airport lounge access pass reseller** | Pay-per-use lounge passes | e.g. Priority Pass, LoungeKey, independent lounges | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Commission per pass sold | 10-15% commission | Lounge network partner agreement | Travellers via airport-guide content sites | Low | Lounge passes sell on impulse before flights |
| **Unaccompanied-minor airport escort service** | Child escort and transfer services | e.g. vetted childcare-trained drivers | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Per-escort fee | 20-30% margin | Child-safeguarding checks and PHV licences | Divorced parents and boarding schools | Low | Parents pay premium for trust; airlines limit UM services |
| **Medical-patient airport transfer coordination** | Non-emergency medical transport from airports | e.g. NEMT providers, accessible vans | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Per-transfer margin | 15-20% margin | NEMT licensing in some states | Medical tourism facilitators and clinics | Low | Medical travellers need reliable coordinated transport |
| **Wedding-guest airport shuttle packages** | Guest shuttles between airport, hotel and venue | e.g. coach and minibus operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Package margin per wedding | 15-20% margin | Coach operator licences | Wedding planners via planner directories | Low | Destination weddings need guest logistics nobody wants to manage |
| **Golf-bag and ski-equipment transfer add-on** | Equipment transport alongside passenger transfers | e.g. courier firms, transfer operators | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-item markup | 15-25% markup | Courier insurance | Golf and ski tour operators | Low | Sports travellers hate dragging gear; add-on lifts order value |
| **Private jet FBO ground transport desk** | Chauffeurs for private aviation terminals | e.g. black-car affiliates | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Markup per ride | 15-20% markup | Chauffeur licences held by affiliates | Private jet brokers and FBO managers | Low | FBO passengers are price-insensitive and recurring |
| **Airport-to-resort transfer for all-inclusive guests** | Resort transfer seats for package holidaymakers | e.g. destination transfer companies | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Per-seat margin | 10-15% per seat | Operator licences in destination | Online travel agencies and resorts | Low | Resorts outsource transfers; package guests prepay |
| **Electric-vehicle-only premium airport transfer brand** | EV chauffeur transfers | e.g. EV chauffeur fleets, Tesla taxi operators | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Markup per ride | 15-20% markup | PHV licences | Corporate sustainability teams and eco travellers | Low | Green branding commands premium with corporate accounts |
| **Multi-airport city transfer comparison site** | Transfer quotes across all airports in a city | e.g. Mozio, Welcome Pickups, local firms | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Commission per booking | 10-15% commission | None | Travellers via comparison SEO | Low | Comparison intent converts; suppliers compete for the slot |
| **Child-seat guaranteed family airport transfers** | Family transfers with pre-fitted child seats | e.g. family-friendly transfer operators | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Markup per transfer | 15-25% markup | Child-seat safety regulations | Family travel bloggers and resorts | Low | Taxis rarely guarantee seats; parents pay for certainty |

## Background Checks & Screening

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Tenant-screening reseller for landlords** | Credit and eviction reports | e.g. consumer reporting agencies | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Markup per report (FCRA compliance) | 40-60% markup | FCRA and permissible-purpose rules | Small landlords via local landlord associations | Low | Small landlords lack direct bureau access and pay for convenience |
| **Employment background check for SMEs** | Criminal and employment verifications | e.g. CRA wholesale APIs | [opencats/OpenCATS](https://github.com/opencats/OpenCATS), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-check markup (FCRA) | 40-60% markup | FCRA and state ban-the-box rules | Small employers via staffing and HR consultants | Low | SMEs want one-stop hiring checks without enterprise contracts |
| **Drug-testing program for trucking fleets** | DOT drug and alcohol tests | e.g. labs, collection sites, TPAs | [frappe/hrms](https://github.com/frappe/hrms), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-test margin (DOT C/TPA rules) | 25-35% per test | DOT 49 CFR Part 40 compliance | Small fleets via trucking associations | Low | Small fleets must run random pools but lack admin capacity |
| **Volunteer screening for churches and sports** | Volunteer background checks | e.g. CRAs | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Per-check fee (FCRA) | 40-50% markup | FCRA disclosure and consent | Churches and youth leagues via associations | Low | Child-safety insurance requirements make checks mandatory |

## Banking-as-a-Service & Cards

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Branded debit card for a creator community** | Program-managed debit cards | e.g. BaaS sponsor banks, program managers | [blnkfinance/blnk](https://github.com/blnkfinance/blnk), [keycloak/keycloak](https://github.com/keycloak/keycloak) | Interchange share (sponsor bank) | 20-40% of interchange | Sponsor-bank program approval; KYC | Creator networks and membership communities | High | Creators monetise audiences through branded financial products |
| **Business banking for freelancers niche** | Embedded accounts | e.g. BaaS platforms | [tigerbeetle/tigerbeetle](https://github.com/tigerbeetle/tigerbeetle), [blnkfinance/blnk](https://github.com/blnkfinance/blnk) | Interchange plus subscription (bank partner) | Subscription plus interchange share | Sponsor-bank partnership; KYC/AML | Freelancers via niche communities and invoicing tools | High | Niche-specific features beat generic neobank accounts for freelancers |
| **Corporate expense cards for SMEs** | Virtual and physical cards | e.g. card issuing platforms | [blnkfinance/blnk](https://github.com/blnkfinance/blnk), [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext) | Interchange share | Interchange share 0.5-1.5% | Issuer program approval; KYC/KYB | SMEs via accountants and bookkeepers | High | Accountants recommend cards that sync spend to ledgers |
| **Prepaid payroll cards for unbanked workers** | Payroll cards | e.g. prepaid program managers | [frappe/hrms](https://github.com/frappe/hrms), [blnkfinance/blnk](https://github.com/blnkfinance/blnk) | Load and interchange fees (regulated) | Per-card fees plus interchange | Prepaid-card and wage-payment regulations | Staffing agencies and seasonal employers | Medium | Employers cut check costs while unbanked workers get pay access |
| **Credit-union core banking hosting** | Hosted core banking | e.g. cloud servers | [apache/fineract](https://github.com/apache/fineract), [openMF/web-app](https://github.com/openMF/web-app) | Per-member monthly | 40-50% gross margin | Credit-union regulator supervision | Small credit unions and cooperatives | High | Small institutions need affordable cores with local support |
| **Open-banking data aggregation reseller** | Account data APIs | e.g. aggregators | [OpenBankProject/OBP-API](https://github.com/OpenBankProject/OBP-API), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-connection fee | 30-50% markup | Open-banking provider licensing via partner | Fintech startups and lenders | Medium | Startups buy aggregation access without negotiating bank connections |
| **Fleet fuel card reseller** | Fuel cards | e.g. fuel card issuers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | Per-gallon rebate share | 2-5 cents per gallon | none | Small fleets via trucking associations | Low | Card issuers pay resellers recurring rebates on fuel volume |

## Corporate Onboarding & LMS

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **White-label LMS for HR consultancies** | Hosted LMS instances | e.g. self-hosted LMS on cloud VPS | [moodle/moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle), [overhangio/tutor](https://github.com/overhangio/tutor) | Monthly fee per tenant | 55-65% gross margin | Learner data-protection rules | HR consultancies via professional networks | Medium | Consultancies resell training platforms under their brand |
| **Restaurant-chain onboarding course packs** | Front-of-house onboarding modules | e.g. hospitality content vendors | [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms), [chamilo/chamilo-lms](https://github.com/chamilo/chamilo-lms) | Per-location subscription | 55-65% gross margin | Food-safety training rules | Multi-unit restaurant operators | Low | High turnover makes fast onboarding valuable |
| **Retail new-hire microlearning on mobile** | Mobile microlearning library | e.g. microlearning publishers | [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Per-employee monthly fee | 55-65% gross margin | none | Retailers via HR tech partners | Low | Frontline staff learn on phones during short breaks |
| **Custom onboarding video production plus LMS hosting** | Video course production and hosting | e.g. freelance video editors | [Chocobozzz/PeerTube](https://github.com/Chocobozzz/PeerTube), [moodle/moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle) | Project fee plus hosting | 40-50% gross margin | Employee-likeness release | SMEs via HR consultants | Low | Production plus hosting gives a one-vendor solution |
| **Extended-enterprise training for SaaS customers** | Customer academy platform | e.g. hosted LMS, content writers | [openedx/edx-platform](https://github.com/openedx/edx-platform), [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse) | Annual platform license | 55-65% gross margin | Learner data-protection rules | SaaS customer-success teams | Medium | Customer academies lower support tickets and churn |
| **Channel-partner certification portal service** | Partner training platform | e.g. LMS hosting | [moodle/moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle), [keycloak/keycloak](https://github.com/keycloak/keycloak) | Per-partner seat fee | 55-65% gross margin | none | Software vendors with reseller channels | Medium | Certified partners sell more and need less support |

## Corporate Travel Management

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **SME corporate travel desk under own brand** | Flights, hotels, cars via TMC tools | e.g. host TMCs, GDS access, bedbanks | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Per-trip transaction fee $15-40 | Plus 5-10% hotel commission | Seller-of-travel registration | SMEs via accountants and chambers of commerce | Low | Personal service with SME rates big TMCs ignore |
| **Event room-block management for associations** | Annual conference room blocks | e.g. convention hotels | [indico/indico](https://github.com/indico/indico), [Qloapps/QloApps](https://github.com/Qloapps/QloApps) | Hotel commission plus attrition management | 7-12% hotel commission | Contract and attrition liability | Trade associations via meeting planners | Medium | Attrition expertise protects associations from penalty clauses |
| **Travel-policy-compliant booking portal for NGOs** | NGO airfares and lodging | e.g. humanitarian fare consolidators | [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Management fee per trip | 8-12% of booking value | Seller-of-travel registration | International NGOs via procurement networks | Medium | Humanitarian fares and donor-compliant reporting in one portal |
| **Sports-league travel management** | Team charter flights and hotels | e.g. charter brokers, hotel chains | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Annual management fee | 5-10% of travel spend | Charter broker rules | Amateur leagues and club federations | Medium | Complex itineraries and last-minute changes suit specialists |
| **Construction-crew travel and lodging desk** | Crew flights and extended stays | e.g. extended-stay brands, airlines | [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext), [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase) | Per-crew-week fee | 8-15% of spend | Seller-of-travel registration | Contractors on remote projects via direct sales | Low | Extended-stay deals cut per-night costs for long projects |
| **Travel expense reconciliation service** | Expense audit and reporting | e.g. card feeds, TMCs | [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Per-report fee | 50-60% gross margin | Financial data privacy | Finance teams of mid-size firms | Low | Catches duplicate and out-of-policy spend automatically |
| **Duty-of-care traveler tracking add-on** | Traveler risk alerts | e.g. risk intelligence vendors | [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-traveler monthly fee | 40-55% gross margin | Employee location privacy | Firms with international staff via HR | Medium | Legal duty-of-care pressure makes budgets available |
| **Law-firm and consultancy travel desk** | Executive travel | e.g. luxury hotel programs, airlines | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Service fee plus hotel commission | 10-15% total take | Seller-of-travel registration | Law and consulting partners via referrals | Low | White-glove service with preferred hotel perks |
| **University faculty travel booking program** | Academic conference travel | e.g. TMC hosts, bedbanks | [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext), [indico/indico](https://github.com/indico/indico) | Per-booking fee | 8-12% of booking value | Public procurement rules | University departments via procurement | Low | Grant-compliant booking records simplify audits |
| **Film-production travel coordination agency** | Cast and crew travel | e.g. airlines, hotels, car rental | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [nextcloud/server](https://github.com/nextcloud/server) | Production management fee | 8-12% of travel spend | Seller-of-travel registration | Production companies via line producers | Low | Fluid shooting schedules need constant itinerary changes |
| **Unused-ticket credit recovery service** | Airline credit management | e.g. airline credit data | [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n), [nocodb/nocodb](https://github.com/nocodb/nocodb) | Percentage of recovered value | 15-25% of recovered credit | Airline credit terms | Companies with stranded credits via finance teams | Low | Pure contingency fee with no upfront risk for clients |
| **Remote-team offsite planning agency** | Offsite venues plus travel | e.g. retreat venues, DMCs | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Planning fee 10-15% | 10-15% of spend | Seller-of-travel registration | Remote-first startups via HR leads | Low | Distributed teams need one coordinator across timezones |
| **Rotational oil-and-gas crew travel** | Crew rotation flights | e.g. marine and energy fare desks | [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Per-movement fee | 6-10% of fare spend | Seller-of-travel registration | Energy contractors via procurement | Medium | Rotations need specialised fares and visa logistics |
| **Speaker-bureau travel handling** | Speaker flights and hotels | e.g. airlines, hotels | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Handling fee per engagement | 10-15% of travel cost | Seller-of-travel registration | Speaker bureaus via direct outreach | Low | Bureaus hate logistics; outsourcing frees them to sell |

| **Corporate travel loyalty-points pooling program** | Points and miles pooling | e.g. airline and hotel loyalty programs | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Subscription plus redemption fee | 40-55% gross margin | Loyalty program terms | SMEs via finance leads | Low | Firms capture points currently lost to employee accounts |

## Courier & Last-Mile Delivery

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **White-label same-day delivery for florists** | Courier capacity | e.g. local courier fleets, gig drivers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [VROOM-Project/vroom](https://github.com/VROOM-Project/vroom) | Per-delivery markup | 25-40% over driver cost | Courier insurance | Florists via supplier and Instagram outreach | Low | Peak holidays need guaranteed same-day capacity |
| **Pharmacy prescription delivery service** | Rx deliveries | e.g. contracted drivers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Per-delivery fee (HIPAA) | 25-35% over driver cost | HIPAA and pharmacy delivery rules | Independent pharmacies via wholesalers | Low | Independent pharmacies compete on convenience with chains |
| **Restaurant delivery cooperative brand** | Delivery drivers | e.g. independent drivers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [opensourcepos/opensourcepos](https://github.com/opensourcepos/opensourcepos) | Per-order fee below aggregator rates | 10-15% of order value | Commercial auto insurance | Local restaurants via owner groups | Medium | Restaurants keep margin that aggregators take |
| **Legal-document courier for courts** | Court filing runs | e.g. local couriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [zammad/zammad](https://github.com/zammad/zammad) | Per-run fee | 30-45% over driver cost | Process-server rules where needed | Law firms via paralegals | Low | Deadline-driven filings need guaranteed on-time runs |
| **Medical lab specimen courier** | Specimen transport | e.g. trained couriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Per-route contract | 20-30% gross margin | Biohazard transport training | Labs and clinics by contract | Medium | Cold-chain logging proves specimen integrity |
| **Grocery delivery for rural towns** | Grocery runs | e.g. local stores | [openfoodfoundation/openfoodnetwork](https://github.com/openfoodfoundation/openfoodnetwork), [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase) | Delivery fee plus markup | 15-25% gross margin | Food-handling rules | Rural residents via local stores | Low | Big apps skip small towns |
| **Cargo-bike delivery for city centers** | E-cargo bike couriers | e.g. bike courier collectives | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [VROOM-Project/vroom](https://github.com/VROOM-Project/vroom) | Per-drop fee | 20-30% gross margin | Low-emission zone rules | Urban retailers and caterers | Medium | Bikes beat vans in congestion zones |
| **Parcel locker network reseller** | Smart lockers | e.g. locker manufacturers | [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Per-parcel fee | 30-45% per-parcel margin | Site and electrical permits | Apartment buildings and carriers | High | Locker use cuts failed-delivery costs |

## Energy & Utility Brokering

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Commercial electricity broker in deregulated states** | Retail electricity supply contracts for businesses | e.g. Constellation, NRG, Direct Energy broker programs | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Per-kWh adder paid monthly by supplier | $0.002-0.006 per kWh residual | Broker license in TX, IL, PA, others | Restaurants and small manufacturers via door-to-door and referrals | Low | Residuals compound across 3-5 year contracts you keep renewing |
| **Natural-gas procurement for manufacturers** | Wholesale-indexed gas supply contracts | e.g. gas marketers, Shell Energy, regional LDC choice programs | [espocrm/espocrm](https://github.com/espocrm/espocrm), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Per-therm commission paid by marketer | $0.01-0.03 per therm | State gas-broker registration in some states | Plants using boilers and furnaces via trade associations | Low | Price volatility makes hedged contracts an easy sell |
| **Community solar subscription sales** | Community solar farm shares to households | e.g. Nexamp, Arcadia, local community solar developers | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic) | Per-subscriber bounty $100-300 plus trailing | $150-400 per enrolled household | State community-solar consumer rules | Renters and HOAs in NY, MN, IL via canvassing | Low | No panels needed so renters convert fast with 5-10% bill savings |
| **Utility bill audit and refund service** | Historical overcharge recovery and rate corrections | Freelance utility auditors, rate-tariff databases | [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Contingency share of refunds recovered | 30-50% of refunds | None beyond standard contracts | Hospitals and multi-site retailers via cold email | Low | Zero-risk pitch since clients pay only when money comes back |
| **Telecom expense management for enterprises** | Telecom invoice audits and contract optimization | Carrier billing data, telecom master agents | [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase), [glpi-project/glpi](https://github.com/glpi-project/glpi) | Share of savings or monthly per-line fee | 25-40% of first-year savings | None | CFOs of 200-2000 employee firms via LinkedIn | Low | Carrier billing errors run 10-15% so savings are nearly guaranteed |

| **Demand-response aggregator for SMEs** | Grid flexibility enrollment and curtailment events | e.g. Enel X, CPower, Voltus partner programs | [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Share of capacity payments earned by clients | 20-35% of incentive payments | ISO market participation via aggregator | Cold storage and manufacturers in PJM territory | Medium | Clients get paid to do nothing most of the year |

| **Energy-efficiency LED retrofit sales** | LED fixtures with partner installation | e.g. Signify, Acuity, lighting distributors, electricians | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | Product and install margin plus rebates | 25-40% gross margin | Electrical contractor license for installs | Warehouses and parking garages via utility rebate lists | Medium | Utility rebates often cover half the project cost |
| **Carbon-credit offset reseller for SMEs** | Verified carbon credits in small lots | e.g. Verra and Gold Standard registries, project developers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Per-tonne markup on wholesale credits | 15-30% markup per tonne | Registry account and retirement documentation | E-commerce brands and event organizers via content | Low | Wholesale lots are too large for SMEs to buy directly |
| **Heat-pump sales agency** | Heat pumps installed by partner HVAC firms | e.g. Mitsubishi, Daikin distributors, local installers | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-install referral commission | $500-1,500 per installed system | HVAC license sits with installer partners | Homeowners replacing furnaces via Google Ads | Low | Rebates and tax credits up to $8,000 drive urgency |
| **Smart-meter energy monitoring service** | Submeters and dashboards for building operators | e.g. Shelly, Emporia, Eastron meter vendors | [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard), [grafana/grafana](https://github.com/grafana/grafana) | Hardware margin plus monthly dashboard fee | 30% hardware, $20-100 per site monthly | Electrical install permits | Gyms and restaurants via facility managers | Medium | Visible waste pays for itself within months |
| **Moving-concierge utility setup service** | Electric, gas, internet and water connections | e.g. utilities, ISPs, retail energy providers' referral programs | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-connection bounty from providers | $20-100 per activated service | Energy broker license in some states | Apartment leasing offices and realtors via partnerships | Low | Free to movers and leasing offices love one less task |
| **Multi-site franchise energy reverse-auction procurement** | Bundled supply contracts across franchise locations | e.g. retail energy suppliers bidding via auction desks | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Per-kWh adder on aggregated load | $0.001-0.004 per kWh | Broker license in deregulated states | Franchisee associations via franchise conventions | Low | Aggregated load wins pricing single stores never see |
| **Municipal streetlight and facility energy aggregation consultant** | Joint-purchase energy contracts for towns | e.g. retail suppliers, municipal aggregation programs | [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Consulting fee plus per-kWh adder | $0.001-0.002 per kWh | Public procurement rules and broker license | Town managers via county associations | Low | Small towns lack staff to run competitive solicitations |
| **REC and green-tariff bundles for marketing claims** | Renewable energy certificates with marketing kit | e.g. Green-e certified REC marketers, 3Degrees | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Markup per MWh plus badge subscription | 20-35% markup | FTC Green Guides claim substantiation | Breweries and DTC brands via sustainability consultants | Low | Brands want the claim, not procurement complexity |
| **Commercial battery storage subscription sales agency** | Behind-the-meter batteries under storage-as-a-service | e.g. Stem, Tesla Megapack integrators, financiers | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Origination fee per kW contracted | $50-150 per kW | Interconnection and electrical permits via installer | Manufacturers with high demand charges via utility data | Medium | Demand-charge savings fund the subscription with no capex |
| **Propane and heating-oil buying club broker** | Negotiated bulk fuel pricing for households | e.g. Suburban Propane, AmeriGas, local dealers | [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja), [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic) | Per-gallon rebate from dealer | $0.05-0.15 per gallon | None | Rural homeowners via Facebook groups and churches | Low | Dealers pay for guaranteed volume in slow seasons |
| **Cold-storage tariff and demand-charge optimization** | Rate-class changes and load-shifting plans | Utility tariff data, metering vendors | [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Share of verified bill savings | 25-40% of savings | None | Cold-storage warehouses and ice rinks via trade shows | Low | Wrong rate class is common and costs tens of thousands |
| **EV fleet charging-as-a-service sales agency** | Depot chargers plus managed charging subscriptions | e.g. bp pulse, Electrada, charging-as-a-service providers | [steve-community/steve](https://github.com/steve-community/steve), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | Commission per charger port contracted | $300-1,000 per port | Electrical permits via installer partner | Delivery and shuttle fleets via fleet managers | Low | Fleets electrify on mandates but hate owning infrastructure |
| **ESCO energy-performance contract referral broker** | Guaranteed-savings retrofit contracts | e.g. Ameresco, Johnson Controls, regional ESCOs | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx) | Referral fee on project value | 1-3% of project value | None | School districts and hospitals via facility directors | Low | Projects are $1M+ so one deal funds a year |
| **Submetering and tenant utility billing for landlords** | Meters plus monthly tenant bill-back service | e.g. Inovonics, Next Century submeter vendors | [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Per-unit monthly billing fee | $2-5 per unit monthly | State submetering and PUC rules | Multifamily owners via property management firms | Medium | Landlords recover 15-25% of utility costs they absorbed |
| **Water-efficiency retrofit sales agency for hotels** | Low-flow fixtures with partner plumbers | e.g. Niagara, Sloan distributors, plumbing contractors | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Product margin plus share of rebates | 25-35% gross margin | Plumbing license sits with installer | Hotel GMs and senior-living operators via rebate programs | Low | Water rates rise yearly and payback is under two years |
| **Energy-audit lead generation for efficiency contractors** | Qualified audit appointments | Independent contractors buying leads | [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | Per-appointment fee to contractors | $50-200 per appointment | Telemarketing and TCPA consent rules | Insulation and HVAC contractors via industry groups | Low | Rebate programs create demand contractors cannot market to |
| **Texas residential electricity plan comparison site** | Retail electricity plan enrollments | e.g. Gexa, TXU, Reliant affiliate programs | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-enrollment affiliate commission | $30-100 per enrollment | PUCT aggregator or broker registration | Movers via realtor and apartment locator partnerships | Low | Texans must choose a plan so intent is built in |
| **Standby-generator sales and service-plan agency** | Home generators with partner install and maintenance | e.g. Generac, Kohler dealer programs, electricians | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Unit margin plus annual service plan | 15-25% unit, 40% service plan | Electrical and gas permits via installer | Suburban homeowners after outages via local ads | Medium | Every storm season creates urgent demand |
| **Building energy-management SaaS reseller for property managers** | White-labeled BEMS dashboards | e.g. BuildingOS-type platforms, sensor OEMs | [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard), [grafana/grafana](https://github.com/grafana/grafana) | Per-building monthly subscription | 30-50% SaaS margin | None | Commercial property managers via BOMA chapters | Low | Benchmarking laws in big cities force adoption |
| **Utility rebate and incentive filing service for contractors** | Rebate application preparation and tracking | Utility and state incentive programs | [paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx](https://github.com/paperless-ngx/paperless-ngx), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-application fee or percent of rebate | 10-15% of rebate value | None | HVAC and lighting contractors via distributor counters | Low | Contractors leave rebates unclaimed because paperwork is tedious |
| **Behind-the-meter power broker for bitcoin miners** | Stranded gas and surplus power contracts | Gas producers, independent power producers | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Per-MWh brokerage fee | $0.50-2 per MWh | State power-marketing and siting rules | Mining hosts via industry conferences | Low | Miners chase cheap power anywhere and move fast |
| **Fleet fuel-card and bulk-fuel procurement broker** | Fuel cards and bulk diesel contracts | e.g. WEX, Fuelman, regional fuel distributors | [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Per-gallon rebate share | $0.02-0.05 per gallon | None | Landscaping and construction fleets via local outreach | Low | Fuel is the second-largest fleet cost and rarely negotiated |

## Event Ticketing & Resale

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **White-label ticketing for small venues** | Branded online box office and door scanning | Self-hosted ticketing software, Stripe-type processors | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [HiEventsDev/Hi.Events](https://github.com/HiEventsDev/Hi.Events) | Per-ticket fee added to buyer price | $0.50-2 per ticket | Sales tax and refund-disclosure rules | Music venues and theatres via local promoter network | Low | Venues keep customer data instead of handing it to Ticketmaster |
| **Authorized ticket broker for sports and concerts** | Secondary-market tickets sourced legally | e.g. Ticketmaster Resale, StubHub, team broker programs | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Spread between buy and sell price | 10-25% spread per ticket | State broker licenses, BOTS Act, resale caps | Corporate clients and concierges via referrals | Medium | Pricing data and relationships beat casual flippers |
| **Hospitality package reseller for major events** | Official VIP hospitality packages | e.g. On Location, official tournament hospitality agents | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Package margin on wholesale allocation | 10-20% package margin | Official sub-agent agreement, seller-of-travel rules | Corporate sales teams via LinkedIn and golf clubs | Medium | Companies buy experiences for clients regardless of price |
| **Festival ticketing with cashless wristbands** | Ticketing plus RFID wristband top-ups | e.g. RFID wristband vendors, payment processors | [alfio-event/alf.io](https://github.com/alfio-event/alf.io), [opensourcepos/opensourcepos](https://github.com/opensourcepos/opensourcepos) | Per-ticket fee plus top-up percentage | 2-4% of cashless spend | Payment processing and unclaimed-balance rules | Regional festivals via production companies | Medium | Cashless lifts on-site spend 15-25% so organizers share gains |
| **Conference registration platform for associations** | Registration, badges and abstract management | Self-hosted event software, badge printer vendors | [indico/indico](https://github.com/indico/indico), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Per-registrant fee or flat event fee | $3-8 per registrant | Data-protection rules for attendee data | Professional associations via conference chairs | Low | Associations churn from expensive SaaS when fees rise |
| **Church and community event ticketing** | Simple ticketing for fundraisers and dinners | Self-hosted ticketing software | [Attendize/Attendize](https://github.com/Attendize/Attendize), [HiEventsDev/Hi.Events](https://github.com/HiEventsDev/Hi.Events) | Flat per-event fee | $50-200 per event | Charity raffle rules in some states | Churches and PTAs via denominational networks | Low | Nonprofits want zero per-ticket fees and plain tools |
| **Corporate event ticket allocation service** | Employee and client event tickets | Ticket brokers, team group-sales desks | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [keycloak/keycloak](https://github.com/keycloak/keycloak) | Management fee plus ticket markup | 8-15% of ticket spend | None | HR and sales teams at mid-size firms via referrals | Low | Companies waste suite tickets without allocation tooling |
| **Theatre group-sales agency** | Discounted group blocks for theatre shows | Theatre group-sales desks, touring producers | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | Group commission paid by theatre | 10-15% of group sales | None | Schools, senior centers and tour operators via outreach | Low | Theatres outsource group sales they lack staff for |
| **Season-ticket share marketplace** | Fractional season tickets split by game | Season-ticket holders listing seats | [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Transaction fee on each split | 8-12% per transaction | Team resale policies and state resale rules | Fans in one city via team subreddits | Low | Holders want cost-sharing without strangers in group chats |
| **Ticketing for marathons and races** | Race registration with timing integration | Timing companies, race directors | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [traccar/traccar](https://github.com/traccar/traccar) | Per-registrant fee | $2-5 per registrant | Waiver and insurance requirements | Local running clubs via race directors | Low | Race directors are volunteers who need turnkey systems |
| **Nightclub guestlist and table booking** | Table reservations and guestlist spots | Clubs and promoters | [HiEventsDev/Hi.Events](https://github.com/HiEventsDev/Hi.Events), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Booking commission on table minimums | 10-15% of table minimum | Alcohol-promotion rules locally | Clubs in one nightlife district via promoters | Low | Promoters already resell tables on WhatsApp without tools |
| **Virtual event ticketing with streaming** | Paid stream access with ticket gating | Self-hosted streaming servers, CDNs | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [owncast/owncast](https://github.com/owncast/owncast) | Per-ticket fee on paid streams | 5-10% per ticket | Content licensing for streamed performances | Theatres and conference organizers via existing clients | Low | Owning the stream avoids platform take rates of 20-30% |
| **School-play and graduation ticketing** | Reserved seating for school events | School districts and PTAs | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [alfio-event/alf.io](https://github.com/alfio-event/alf.io) | Per-ticket fee or annual school fee | $0.50-1 per ticket | Student data privacy rules | High-school drama teachers via district offices | Low | Schools still sell paper tickets at the door |
| **Esports tournament ticketing and passes** | Spectator tickets and competitor passes | Tournament organizers and venues | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse) | Per-ticket fee | 5-8% per ticket | Prize-competition rules for passes | Collegiate esports clubs via Discord | Low | Organizers need combined ticketing and bracket check-in |
| **Box-office outsourcing for touring shows** | Box office staff and ticketing systems | Promoters and touring productions | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [opensourcepos/opensourcepos](https://github.com/opensourcepos/opensourcepos) | Per-show fee plus per-ticket | $500-2,000 per show | Local amusement tax collection | Regional promoters via venue managers | Low | Promoters rent box office only on show nights |
| **Comedy-club ticketing with dynamic pricing** | Branded ticketing with demand-based pricing | Self-hosted ticketing, pricing scripts | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-ticket fee plus share of uplift | $1-2 per ticket plus 10% uplift | Price-display transparency laws | Independent comedy clubs via comedian managers | Low | Clubs leave money on Saturday shows and empty Tuesdays |
| **Museum timed-entry ticketing reseller** | Timed admission slots with capacity control | Museums and attractions | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [Qloapps/QloApps](https://github.com/Qloapps/QloApps) | Per-ticket fee plus monthly | $0.30-1 per ticket | Accessibility requirements for online sales | Regional museums via museum associations | Low | Timed entry became standard and legacy systems cannot do it |
| **Food and wine festival ticket packages** | Festival passes bundled with tastings and hotels | Festival organizers, wineries, bedbanks | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Package markup over components | 15-25% package margin | Alcohol event permits held by organizer | Foodie subscribers via newsletter and Instagram | Low | Bundles sell at premium and fill hotel partners |
| **Ticket refund-protection add-on for organizers** | Refund protection sold at checkout | e.g. Protect Group, XCover, insurance partners | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Revenue share on protection fees | 20-40% of protection premium | Insurance distribution rules, partner carries license | Festival and conference organizers via ticketing clients | Low | Organizers earn new revenue with zero refund disputes |
| **Rodeo and county fair ticketing with gate POS** | Admission, grandstand and carnival ticketing | Fair boards and rodeo associations | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [opensourcepos/opensourcepos](https://github.com/opensourcepos/opensourcepos) | Per-ticket fee plus hardware rental | $0.50-1 per ticket | Local amusement taxes | Fair boards via state fair associations | Medium | Fairs run on cash and clipboards and lose revenue |
| **Fan-club presale ticketing for artists** | Verified-fan presale windows and memberships | Artist management and promoters | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [keycloak/keycloak](https://github.com/keycloak/keycloak) | Membership fee plus per-ticket | $2-5 per presale ticket | Consumer protection on membership terms | Mid-size touring artists via managers | Low | Artists want fans not bots to get first access |
| **Cinema ticketing white-label for independent theaters** | Online seat selection and concessions pre-order | Independent cinemas, film distributors | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [opensourcepos/opensourcepos](https://github.com/opensourcepos/opensourcepos) | Per-ticket convenience fee | $0.50-1.50 per ticket | Film distributor reporting requirements | Arthouse cinemas via regional film societies | Low | Indie cinemas pay high fees to chains' ticketing vendors |
| **Ski lift ticket and lesson reseller** | Discounted lift tickets and lesson bundles | e.g. Liftopia-style wholesalers, resort partner programs | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Commission on resort wholesale rates | 8-15% commission | Resort reseller agreement | Ski clubs and corporate groups via newsletters | Low | Resorts push advance sales to smooth capacity |
| **Authorized theme-park ticket reseller for travel agents** | Discounted park tickets at net rates | e.g. Disney, Universal authorized ticket wholesalers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Margin between net and gate price | 5-12% margin | Authorized seller agreement, no resale of used tickets | Family travel agents via host agencies | Low | Agents need one source for multiple parks |
| **Zoo and aquarium membership sales outsourcing** | Annual memberships and ticket upsells | Zoos and aquariums | [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Commission on memberships sold | 10-20% of membership value | Charitable solicitation registration if nonprofit | Mid-size zoos via AZA conferences | Low | Memberships drive repeat visits but gates lack sales staff |
| **Ticket-scanning and access-control crew outsourcing** | Trained scanning staff with devices | Event staffing agencies, scanner hardware vendors | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [kimai/kimai](https://github.com/kimai/kimai) | Per-staff-hour markup | 25-40% on labor | Event staffing and security licensing locally | Festival producers via production companies | Low | Producers struggle to staff and train gates |
| **Pop-up dinner and supper-club ticketing brand** | Ticketed chef dinners in unusual venues | Chefs, private venues | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [mautic/mautic](https://github.com/mautic/mautic) | Per-seat commission | 15-20% per seat | Temporary food permits held by chef | Foodies via Instagram and local newsletters | Low | Chefs want audiences without running their own marketing |
| **Charity gala ticketing with auction software** | Tables, tickets and silent auctions | Nonprofits and event planners | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [civicrm/civicrm-core](https://github.com/civicrm/civicrm-core) | Per-event fee plus auction percentage | 3-5% of auction proceeds | Charitable gaming and raffle rules | Development directors via nonprofit associations | Low | Galas pay 5-8% to incumbents and want cheaper |
| **Event parking pre-sale platform near stadiums** | Reserved parking spots from lot owners | Private lots, churches, businesses near venues | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Commission per spot sold | 15-25% per spot | Local parking and zoning permits | Lot owners near one stadium via door knocking | Low | Game-day parking is chaotic and underpriced |
| **Lecture-series and author-event ticketing for bookstores** | Ticket-plus-book bundles | Independent bookstores, publishers | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Per-ticket fee on bundles | $1-2 per ticket | Sales tax on bundled books | Indie bookstores via regional booksellers associations | Low | Bundles guarantee book sales for author events |
| **Seasonal attraction timed-entry ticketing (light shows, pumpkin patches)** | Timed tickets with capacity caps | Seasonal attraction operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [HiEventsDev/Hi.Events](https://github.com/HiEventsDev/Hi.Events) | Per-ticket fee | $0.50-1.50 per ticket | Local assembly and parking permits | Farm attractions and light-show producers via agritourism groups | Low | Operators need timed entry only six weeks a year |
| **Ticket fraud and bot screening service for organizers** | Bot-blocking and fraud-review service | Fraud-screening APIs, CAPTCHA vendors | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [crowdsecurity/crowdsec](https://github.com/crowdsecurity/crowdsec) | Per-ticket screening fee | $0.10-0.30 per ticket | BOTS Act and data-protection rules | High-demand event organizers via ticketing partners | Low | Chargebacks and scalped inventory cost more than screening |

## Fitness Programs & Apps

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **White-label workout app for personal trainers** | Branded training app with programs and tracking | Self-hosted fitness platforms, app-shell developers | [wger-project/wger](https://github.com/wger-project/wger), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Per-trainer monthly SaaS fee | $30-80 per trainer monthly | App store policies | Independent trainers via Instagram outreach | Medium | Trainers want their brand, not a marketplace's |
| **Licensed group-fitness classes for gyms** | Pre-choreographed class programs and music | e.g. Les Mills, Zumba, independent program licensors | [Chocobozzz/PeerTube](https://github.com/Chocobozzz/PeerTube), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-gym monthly license | 30-50% margin on sublicense | Music licensing covered by program | Independent gyms via regional fitness conventions | Low | Gyms fill schedules without choreographing anything |
| **Corporate fitness challenge platform** | Step and activity challenges with leaderboards | Wearable APIs, Fitbit and Garmin partner programs | [wger-project/wger](https://github.com/wger-project/wger), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Per-employee per-challenge fee | $2-5 per employee | Health data privacy rules | HR wellness leads via benefits brokers | Low | Wellness budgets must be spent and challenges are visible |
| **On-demand fitness video library for hotels** | In-room workout video channel | Fitness content licensors, independent instructors | [jellyfin/jellyfin](https://github.com/jellyfin/jellyfin), [Chocobozzz/PeerTube](https://github.com/Chocobozzz/PeerTube) | Per-room monthly license | $0.50-2 per room monthly | Content licensing agreements | Boutique hotel groups via hospitality trade shows | Low | Hotels want wellness amenities without building gyms |
| **Senior fall-prevention exercise program** | Evidence-based balance class curriculum | e.g. Otago, Tai Ji Quan program licensors | [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-participant or per-site fee | $50-150 per participant | Instructor certification by licensor | Senior centers and Medicare Advantage plans via area agencies | Low | Payers fund fall prevention because falls cost $30k each |
| **Postnatal fitness program under own brand** | Licensed postnatal recovery programs | Women's-health trainers, pelvic-floor physios | [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms), [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill) | Monthly subscription | $20-40 per member monthly | Health-claim advertising rules | New mothers via midwife and doula referrals | Low | Underserved niche with strong word-of-mouth |
| **Gym-equipment reseller for home gyms** | Commercial-grade racks, bikes and plates | e.g. Rogue-type distributors, Alibaba factories | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase) | Product margin on drop-shipped equipment | 20-35% gross margin | Product safety standards | Garage-gym enthusiasts via YouTube reviews | Medium | Freight-heavy items keep marketplaces less competitive |
| **Fitness-class aggregator pass** | Multi-studio class credits | Local boutique studios | [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Spread between pass price and studio payout | 15-30% spread per visit | None | Studios in one neighborhood via owner outreach | Medium | Studios accept discounted fills for off-peak classes |
| **Online running coach marketplace** | Coach-written training plans and check-ins | Certified running coaches | [wger-project/wger](https://github.com/wger-project/wger), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission on coaching fees | 20-30% commission | None | Marathon registrants via race partnerships | Low | Race registration lists are perfect intent signals |
| **Golf fitness program licensing** | Golf-specific mobility and strength plans | e.g. TPI-certified trainers, golf fitness educators | [frappe/lms](https://github.com/frappe/lms), [Chocobozzz/PeerTube](https://github.com/Chocobozzz/PeerTube) | Per-member license via golf clubs | $5-10 per member monthly | None | Country clubs via club pros | Low | Clubs sell it as a member perk |
| **Gym-management software reseller** | Member management, billing and check-in SaaS | Open-source ERP, access-control hardware | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [killbill/killbill](https://github.com/killbill/killbill) | Per-gym monthly fee | $50-150 per gym monthly | Card-data compliance via processor | Independent gyms via local networking | Low | Incumbents raised prices and locked contracts |

## Flights & Tour Packages

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Ethnic diaspora consolidator fares (e.g. Nigeria, Philippines)** | Net consolidator airfares on key diaspora routes | e.g. IATA consolidators, GDS access via host agency | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Markup per ticket over net fare | $20-80 per ticket | Seller-of-travel registration in CA, FL, WA, HI | Community churches and grocery stores via flyers and WhatsApp | Low | Communities trust an agent who speaks their language |
| **Packaged honeymoon trips sold under own brand** | Flights, hotel and transfer bundles | e.g. DMCs, Hotelbeds, host agencies | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Package margin over net components | 12-20% package margin | Seller-of-travel registration, package travel regulations | Engaged couples via wedding planners and bridal fairs | Medium | Couples pay for curation and one point of contact |
| **Group study-tour packages for schools** | Educational group tours with flights and guides | e.g. EF-type operators, DMCs, group airfare desks | [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Per-student package margin | 10-15% per student | Package travel rules, safeguarding, school procurement | Teachers via subject associations and school boards | Medium | One teacher relationship yields 30-plus travelers yearly |
| **White-label tour catalogue for travel bloggers** | Branded catalogue of multiday tours | e.g. TourRadar, G Adventures, Intrepid affiliate programs | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Affiliate commission per booking | 8-12% commission | Affiliate disclosure rules | Travel bloggers with 50k-plus audiences via outreach | Low | Bloggers monetize trust without operating anything |
| **Sports-fan travel packages to away games** | Match tickets with flights and hotels | e.g. official club travel partners, bedbanks | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Package markup over components | 15-30% package markup | Seller-of-travel rules, ticket resale laws | Supporter clubs via fan forums and pubs | Medium | Fans travel in groups and rebook every season |
| **Festival travel bundles (Coachella, Tomorrowland)** | Festival passes bundled with lodging and shuttles | e.g. official festival travel partners, bedbanks | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Bundle margin over wholesale | 10-20% bundle margin | Festival reseller authorization, seller-of-travel | Festival-goers via TikTok and fan Discords | Medium | Lodging near festivals sells out so bundles win |

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Margin / take | Regulation | First customers & channel | Cost | Why it works |
